# Tables, list views, sheets and modals: defaulter report (2026-10-27)

Replaces the shorter review committed earlier today. Part 1 (§0–§9) covers tables, list rows, sheets and modals; Part 2 (§10–§25) covers each table page as a whole; Part 3 (§26–§35) covers every page in the app and how to keep it fixed.

**Scope:** every `DataTable` (120 instances in 104 files), its list row
(`mobileRow`), slide-up sheet (`mobileRow.detail` or the auto-built one), expanded panel and
row actions; the 12 side sheets; and every modal (145 files).

**Method:** two parsers read each table's columns, filters, row actions, list row, sheet
fields/actions and expanded render, and each modal's size, inputs, form handling and layout. Rules
were then applied per row. Sections 0, 1, 6, 8 and 9 were checked by hand. Sections 2–5 and 7 are
rule-generated from the code; each row names the exact columns, fields and actions involved.
Known limits: filters or sheet fields built at runtime (hooks, props) cannot be counted statically
and are either skipped or marked "verify".

**Read §0 first.** Four of the list/sheet complaints are one bug each in `DataTable`, and fixing
them there clears most of the rows in §4 and §5 at once.

| Section | Defaulters |
|---|---|
| 0. DataTable itself | 6 |
| 1. Modals that should be side sheets | 16 |
| 2. Unexpanded tables | 89 |
| 3. Expanded panels | 68 |
| 4. List views (phone rows) | 91 |
| 5. Slide-up sheets | 99 |
| 6. Side sheets | 13 |
| 7. Modals | 126 |
| 8. AGENTS.md practices to change | 16 |
| 9. Other | 6 |

---

## 0. `DataTable` itself (fix once, clears most of §4–§5)

| # | Defect | Affects | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **0.1 Card view tap is dead.** In card view, `handleCardClick` calls `toggleExpand` for expandable rows, but the card grid never renders `expandable.render`. The tap does nothing, and the detail sheet is never reached ([data-table.tsx](../../components/ui/data-table/data-table.tsx) ~L1796) | 69 tables (expandable + cardRenderer) | In card view, ignore `canExpand` and open the detail sheet |
| 2 | **0.2 Phone tap expands instead of opening the sheet.** In the list, `handleSelect` checks `canExpand` before `detailConfig`. On a phone the desktop panel is squeezed under the row and the slide-up sheet can't be reached (~L1552) | 73 tables (every expandable table with a list view) | Sheet first. Render `expandable.render(row)` as a section inside the sheet under the fields, so timelines and attachments still reach phones. Keep inline expand only for `mobileRow.expandInline` |
| 3 | **0.3 Row actions never reach the phone.** `rowActions` only render in table view. The list row has none, and the auto-built sheet gets one "Open" button, and only when `onSelect` is passed (~L398–415) | Every table with `rowActions` and no hand-written `detail.actions` (see §5) | Build sheet actions from `rowActions` (respecting `hidden`, `variant`) when `detail.actions` is not given. Primary action first, destructive last |
| 4 | **0.4 Auto-built sheet drops render-only columns.** `detailConfig` uses `columns.filter(c => c.accessor)`. A column with only `render` (status badge, amount, progress) disappears from the sheet | Every table listed in §2 with a 'render but no accessor' defect | Make `accessor` required on data columns (type-level), or fall back to `render` output in the sheet |
| 5 | **0.5 Expanded cell already has padding.** The expanded `TableCell` has `p-4` and a `bg-muted/30` row, and most panels add their own on top (see §3) | ~30 panels | Document that panels render content only. Optionally drop the cell to `px-4 py-3` and add a `DetailList` primitive |
| 6 | **0.6 Bottom sheet is a grid of bordered tiles.** Every field is a `rounded-lg border p-2.5` box with an `text-[11px]` label, so 10 fields are 10 boxes. The header is centred, unlike every side sheet | Every slide-up sheet | Use a plain 2-column `<dl>` with dividers and `text-xs` labels. Keep tiles only for tappable (copy/link) fields. Left-align the header to match side sheets |

---

## 1. Tables whose row action opens a modal but should open a side sheet

Rule: **viewing or reviewing a record → side sheet; creating, editing or confirming → modal.**
Form modals (create/edit) are correct and are reviewed in §7, not here.

| # | Route | Row action | Opens today | Problem | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /admin/hr/employees | View Full Profile / Edit Staff | [EmployeeViewModal.tsx](../../components/employees/EmployeeViewModal.tsx): Dialog, `h-[90vh]`, 3xl↔5xl | Profile, edit form, signature and three inner tabs in one 1,344-line modal. The width jumps between modes and short tabs still fill 90vh | Profile → right side sheet (`sm:max-w-2xl`) with Assets/Tasks/Docs as sheet tabs. Edit → same sheet in edit mode with a sticky footer, or `/admin/hr/employees/[id]/edit`. Signature → its own small dialog |
| 2 | /admin/assets | History | [AssetHistoryDialog.tsx](../../components/assets/AssetHistoryDialog.tsx): Dialog 3xl | A read-only timeline in a centred modal | Side sheet with the timeline as a list |
| 3 | /admin/assets | Issues | [AssetIssuesDialog.tsx](../../components/assets/AssetIssuesDialog.tsx): Dialog 2xl | A list of records plus an add form in a modal | Side sheet: issue list, with 'Report issue' in the footer opening a small form |
| 4 | /admin/documentation | View | [admin-doc-view-dialog.tsx](../../components/documentation/admin-doc-view-dialog.tsx): Dialog 3xl | The same document is a **sheet** on the staff side (`doc-view-dialog`) and a **modal** here | Reuse `doc-view-dialog` (the sheet) and delete the admin copy (one component per screen) |
| 5 | /admin/hr/job-descriptions | View Documentation | [job-description-dialog.tsx](../../app/admin/hr/job-descriptions/_components/job-description-dialog.tsx): Dialog 3xl | Reading a long document in a modal | Side sheet `sm:max-w-2xl` |
| 6 | /admin/settings/roles | View Users | [role-users-dialog.tsx](../../app/admin/settings/roles/_components/role-users-dialog.tsx): Dialog 3xl | A list of members in a modal | Side sheet with the member list; 'Add user' in the footer |
| 7 | /admin/hr/lunch | Viewers | [lunch-menu-viewers-dialog.tsx](../../app/admin/hr/lunch/_components/lunch-menu-viewers-dialog.tsx): Dialog 3xl | A read-only list in a modal | Side sheet |
| 8 | /admin/dev/login-logs | View Access Profile | Inline Dialog 3xl in the page file | Read-only profile/stats in a modal, written inside the page | Side sheet in `_components/access-profile-sheet.tsx` |
| 9 | /admin/pms/peer-feedback | View | Inline Dialog 2xl in the page file | Read-only feedback detail in a modal | Side sheet |
| 10 | /admin/pms/development-plans | View | Inline Dialog in the page file (`viewingPlan`) | Read-only plan detail in a modal | Side sheet; 'Edit' in the footer |
| 11 | /admin/reports/action-tracker | View | Inline Dialog 3xl (department action points) | A list of action points in a modal | Side sheet (`sm:max-w-2xl`) with the points as a list; same component as the staff side |
| 12 | /reports/action-tracker | View Action Points | Inline Dialog 2xl | Same content as the admin page, built twice | Share one sheet component with the admin page |
| 13 | /admin/feedback/surveys | View Details | Inline Dialog xl | A read-only survey response in a modal | Side sheet |
| 14 | /admin/documentation (controlled docs register) | View Acknowledgements | [controlled-doc-acknowledgements-dialog.tsx](../../components/documentation/controlled/controlled-doc-acknowledgements-dialog.tsx): Dialog | A list of who acknowledged, in a modal | Side sheet (or a tab inside the controlled-doc view sheet) |
| 15 | /admin/hr/resources | Review | [booking-review-dialog.tsx](../../app/admin/hr/resources/_components/booking-review-dialog.tsx): Dialog | Reviewing a booking and deciding. Leave and attendance appeals already do this in a sheet | Side sheet with Approve/Reject in a sticky footer, matching leave and appeals |
| 16 | /accounts/payroll | View Payslip | Inline Dialog 2xl | Printable document preview | **Keep as a dialog** (document preview), but move it to `_components/` |

## 2. Unexpanded tables with bad UI

| # | Route | File | Defect | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /accounts/payroll | [view.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/payroll/view.tsx) | 1. Buttons hand-built inside column(s) "Actions" instead of `rowActions` — invisible to the phone list, the slide-up sheet and keyboard nav<br>2. `filters={[]}` — no filters<br>3. Column(s) with `render` but no `accessor`: Gross Cash Pay, Total Deductions, Net Pay — not sortable, not exported, dropped from the auto-built sheet<br>4. No pagination — every row renders at once<br>5. `<DialogContent>` written inside this page file (AGENTS.md prohibition) | 1. Move them into `rowActions` (and `mobileRow.detail.actions`); delete the column<br>2. Add 2 filters on the main categorical columns<br>3. Add an `accessor` returning the plain value<br>4. Add `pagination={{ pageSize: 25 }}` (or `serverSide` if it can exceed ~500 rows)<br>5. Move the dialog(s) to `_components/` |
| 2 | /documentation | [documentation-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/documentation-content.tsx) | 1. No pagination — every row renders at once | 1. Add `pagination={{ pageSize: 25 }}` (or `serverSide` if it can exceed ~500 rows) |
| 3 | /documentation/personal | [personal-documentation-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/personal/personal-documentation-content.tsx) | 1. 5 filters — max is 4 | 1. Drop the least-used; merge start/end into one range filter |

## 3. Expanded panels with bad UI (inside the expanded row)

Panels that only delegate to a component are listed only if that component has a problem.

| # | Route | File | Defect (inside the expanded panel) | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /accounts/assets | [assets-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/assets/assets-content.tsx) | 1. Panel root adds its own padding/border/background (`bg-muted/20 space-y-4 rounded-lg border p-4 text-xs`) inside a cell that already has `p-4` + tinted row → doubled whitespace, box-in-row<br>2. Repeats 5 columns already visible in the row: Unique Code, Model, Serial, Year, Assigned<br>3. 8× text-[9–11px]<br>4. 8 uppercase micro-headings | 1. Strip the root to layout only (`grid gap-…` / `space-y-…`)<br>2. Remove the repeated fields; if nothing new is left, delete `expandable`<br>3. Raise to `text-xs`<br>4. One heading style per section (SectionHeading); drop headings over single fields |
| 2 | /help-desk | [help-desk-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/help-desk/help-desk-content.tsx) | 1. Panel root adds its own padding/border/background (`bg-muted/20 space-y-4 rounded-lg border p-4 text-xs`) inside a cell that already has `p-4` + tinted row → doubled whitespace, box-in-row<br>2. 5× text-[9–11px]<br>3. 5 uppercase micro-headings | 1. Strip the root to layout only (`grid gap-…` / `space-y-…`)<br>2. Raise to `text-xs`<br>3. One heading style per section (SectionHeading); drop headings over single fields |
| 3 | /hr/attendance | [attendance-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/hr/attendance/attendance-content.tsx) | 1. Repeats 3 columns already visible in the row: Clock In, Work Hour, Missed<br>3. 4× text-[9–11px]<br>4. 6 uppercase micro-headings<br>5. 194-line render written inline in the page | 1. Strip the root to layout only (`grid gap-…` / `space-y-…`)<br>2. Remove the repeated fields; keep only what the row cannot show<br>3. Raise to `text-xs`<br>4. One heading style per section (SectionHeading); drop headings over single fields<br>5. Extract to `_components/<name>-expanded.tsx` |

## 4. List views (the phone row)

"Hidden from the list" names the columns carrying status, dates, amounts, people or
categories that the row never shows, which is what pushes people to switch to Table view.
Example: `/admin/dev/impersonation` shows Role twice, never shows Status, and its Impersonate
button (a hand-built column) can't be reached from the list or the sheet.

| # | Route | File | List row shows | Defect | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /accounts/assets | [assets-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/assets/assets-content.tsx) | title · subtitle · trailing | 1. Hidden from the list: Assigned<br>2. text-[9–11px] in the list row | 1. add Assigned to `subtitle`/`meta`<br>2. Raise to `text-xs` |
| 2 | /accounts/payroll | [view.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/payroll/view.tsx) | title · subtitle · trailing | 1. Hidden from the list: Total Deductions | 1. add Total Deductions to `subtitle`/`meta` |
| 3 | /correspondence | [portal-reference-generator-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/correspondence/portal-reference-generator-content.tsx) | title · subtitle · trailing | 1. Hidden from the list: Type, Department<br>2. Same field shown twice: status (title + trailing)<br>3. text-[9–11px] in the list row | 1. add Type, Department to `subtitle`/`meta`<br>2. Replace the duplicate with a field the row is missing<br>3. Raise to `text-xs` |
| 4 | /directory | [directory-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/directory/directory-content.tsx) | title · subtitle · trailing | 1. text-[9–11px] in the list row | 1. Raise to `text-xs` |
| 5 | /documentation | [documentation-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/documentation-content.tsx) | title · subtitle · trailing | 1. Hidden from the list: Updated | 1. add Updated to `subtitle`/`meta` |

## 6. Side sheets with bad UI

| # | Sheet | Used on | Defect | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | [AuditLogDetailPanel.tsx](../../components/audit/AuditLogDetailPanel.tsx) | /admin/audit-logs | 9 bordered boxes (card-in-card), 4 uppercase headings, sub-12px text, 369 lines | Flatten to `divide-y` sections. Show the before/after diff as one table, not one box per field |
| 2 | [requisition-preview-sheet.tsx](../../components/requisitions/requisition-preview-sheet.tsx) | /requisitions, /admin/accounts/requisitions | 9 bordered boxes, 7 uppercase headings | Flatten. One `SectionHeading` per section; line items as a plain table |
| 3 | [appeal-review-dialog.tsx](../../app/admin/hr/attendance/_components/appeal-review-dialog.tsx) | /admin/hr/attendance (Appeals) | 7 boxes. The whole `SheetContent` scrolls (`overflow-y-auto` on the root), so the header and approve/reject buttons scroll away. Header reserves only `pr-6` with a right-aligned badge next to the close button. Narrower (`max-w-md/lg`) than every other sheet. Named *Dialog | `DetailSheet` shell: fixed header, scrolling body, sticky footer, `pr-12`, `sm:max-w-xl`. Rename to `appeal-review-sheet.tsx` |
| 4 | [payment-preview-sheet.tsx](../../components/payments/payment-preview-sheet.tsx) | /accounts/payments, /admin/accounts/payments | 681 lines. Widest sheet (`2xl/3xl`). 5 boxes | Split into sections/components; `sm:max-w-2xl` |
| 5 | [feedback-detail-dialog.tsx](../../components/feedback/feedback-detail-dialog.tsx) | /admin/feedback | 4 boxes. Named *Dialog | Flatten; rename to `feedback-detail-sheet.tsx` |
| 6 | [md-queue-preview-sheet.tsx](../../components/md-desk/md-queue-preview-sheet.tsx) | /admin MD desk | 3 boxes. Width `lg/xl` differs from siblings | Flatten; standard width |
| 7 | [controlled-doc-view-dialog.tsx](../../components/documentation/controlled/controlled-doc-view-dialog.tsx) | /documentation, /admin/documentation | 3 boxes, sub-12px text. Named *Dialog | Flatten; rename |
| 8 | [doc-view-dialog.tsx](../../components/documentation/doc-view-dialog.tsx) | /documentation | **Invalid HTML**: `<div>` inside `SheetDescription` (renders a `<p>`), which gives a hydration warning. No footer/actions (Edit/Delete only in the table menu). Named *Dialog | Move the meta row out of `SheetDescription`; add a footer with Edit/Delete; rename |
| 9 | [UserTaskDetailsDialog.tsx](../../components/tasks/UserTaskDetailsDialog.tsx) | /tasks | 6× sub-12px text. No footer: the task actions are in the body. Named *Dialog | `text-xs`; sticky footer for Submit/Update; rename |
| 10 | [user-ticket-details-dialog.tsx](../../components/help-desk/user-ticket-details-dialog.tsx) | /help-desk | Sub-12px text. No footer. Named *Dialog | Same as above |
| 11 | [event-detail-sheet.tsx](../../components/events/event-detail-sheet.tsx) | /events, /admin events | Root `SheetContent` scrolls, so the header scrolls away. Default padding (no `p-0`) unlike the others. Narrowest width (`sm:max-w-lg`) | `DetailSheet` shell; standard width |
| 12 | [leave-detail-dialog.tsx](../../app/admin/hr/leave/_components/leave-detail-dialog.tsx) | /admin/hr/leave | Layout fixed in this session. Still named *Dialog | Rename to `leave-detail-sheet.tsx`; becomes the reference implementation for `DetailSheet` |
| 13 | **All 12** | — | No shared shell: each rebuilds header/body/footer. Four widths (`lg`, `xl`, `2xl`, `3xl`). Close-button clearance handled ad hoc (`pr-6`, `pr-8`, `pr-12`, none) | Add `components/ui/detail-sheet.tsx`: header (avatar, title, meta, badges with `pr-12`), `divide-y` scrolling body, sticky footer. Default `sm:max-w-xl`, `wide` prop = `2xl` for document previews |

for why its view mode should be a sheet.

## 8. AGENTS.md practices that need updating

| # | AGENTS.md says | What is wrong | Proposed text |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Contacts view: "`contactsView` removes the desktop table from view, and `expandable` never fires in it" | False. `expandable` does fire in the list: the row tap toggles an inline expand (data-table.tsx ~L1553), which is defect 0.2 | "In the list, a row tap opens the slide-up sheet. If the table is expandable, the expanded content renders inside the sheet below the fields." (after fix 0.2) |
| 2 | Feature checklist: "Mobile defaults to list view with horizontal scroll; users opt into card view via the toggle" | Contradicts the Contacts view section (List · Cards · Table, `defaultViewMode {mobile:'contacts'}`). Stale | Delete the row; point to Contacts view |
| 3 | "A row tap opens the sheet — never a modal" | Correct but incomplete. It says nothing about the sheet carrying the row's **actions** (defect 0.3) or its **fields** (0.4). That gap is why the list and sheet hide important things | Add: "The sheet must offer every row action the table offers, and every column the table shows. If you write `detail`, it must cover both." |
| 4 | Mobile list rows: no rule | Nothing says what a list row must show, hence the impersonation-style rows (title + duplicated role, status missing) | Add: "A list row shows title, the 1–2 facts used to tell rows apart (subtitle), and the row's status as a `trailing` badge. Never show the same field twice." |
| 5 | Expanded rows: only "Expandable rows ✅ Pass `expandable`" | No guidance, so 30 panels double-pad, repeat the row's columns, and nest boxes | Add an "Expanded row" standard: content only (no outer padding/border/bg); never repeat a visible column; fields via `DetailList`; boxes only for callouts; extract renders over ~60 lines |
| 6 | Sheet vs modal: only "Dialogs on phones: use `ResponsiveModal` … for forms" | No rule on desktop: viewing a record in a modal is common (§1) | Add: "Viewing or reviewing a record → side sheet (`DetailSheet`). Creating, editing or confirming → modal (`ResponsiveModal`). Long multi-section edit forms → a page." |
| 7 | Stats: "Stats cards — required on every table page" (`StatGrid` of 3–4 `StatCard`s) | Contradicted by Hard prohibitions, which say to prefer `statBadges` line style on lookup and record pages | Make one rule: "`statBadges` (line) by default; add a `StatGrid` band only when the metrics earn desktop space" |
| 8 | Layout order (f): "Table (sticky header, **coloured** header row…)" | Feature checklist says "**Muted** header row `bg-muted/80` — do not change" | Say "muted header row" in both places |
| 9 | Hard prohibition: "Fewer than 2 filter options on any table page" | Absolute. A 3-row lookup table (payment processors) gains nothing from two filters; the rule just produces filler filters | "…on any table that can exceed ~20 rows" |
| 10 | "Minimum text size is `text-xs` (12px). No `text-[10px]` / `text-[11px]`" | The rule is right but unenforced: 80 of 104 table files and 55 modal files break it, and so does `DataTable`'s own sheet | Keep the rule. Add an ESLint `no-restricted-syntax` / tailwind lint rule for `text-[9-11px]` so it fails `lint:strict` |
| 11 | "Dialogs on phones: use `ResponsiveModal`" | 4 of 145 modal files comply. Unenforced | Keep; add a lint rule banning `DialogContent` imports outside `components/ui` and `ResponsiveModal` |
| 12 | "Forms use react-hook-form + zod" | 13 of ~100 form modals do. Unenforced, and silent on migrating | Keep; add "when you touch a hand-rolled form modal, migrate it" (same as the 1,000-line rule) |
| 13 | Back link convention: every `/admin/…` page links back to its section | No recorded rationale (bulk sync commit `7283d7fb`). On sidebar destinations it duplicates the sidebar and costs a header line; the labels have drifted into 8+ wordings (§23) | "Pages the sidebar opens directly have no back link. Nested pages link to their parent as 'Back to <parent's sidebar name>'." |
| 14 | Feature checklist: "List / card view toggle ✅ Pass `viewToggle` + `cardRenderer`" | Encourages a third view mode that repeats the list on ~110 tables, and its tap is broken on expandable tables (§15) | "Card view is opt-in for pages where a card shows what a row cannot (summaries, charts). Default toggle: List · Table." |
| 15 | Export placement: "use `ExportOptionsDialog`" | The built-in `DataTable` `export` (filtered + sorted rows, zero page code) exists but lands in the toolbar, against this same rule; two other export dialogs also exist (§13, §24) | Pick one: built-in `export`, rendered into the header slot. Update this section to say so |
| 16 | Commit Message Standard: "Use a single-line … subject. Keep the **full message** to 72 characters or fewer" | Wrong: commitlint only limits the **header** (`header-max-length: 72`); bodies are allowed and used | "Keep the subject line to 72 characters or fewer; add a body for the why when it helps" |

---

## 9. Anything else

| # | Area | Defect | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | .husky/commit-msg | Pipes commitlint to `/dev/null` and prints a generic "invalid format" message, so a too-long header looks like a format error. It also points to `COMMIT_GUIDE.md`, which does not exist | Print commitlint's own output; drop the dead reference |
| 2 | Staff vs admin duplicates | The same screen is built twice: staff leave panel vs admin leave sections, staff vs admin action-tracker dialogs, staff vs admin documentation viewers. This breaks the one-component-per-screen rule | Staff leave → reuse `leave-detail-sections.tsx`; one action-points sheet; one doc-view sheet |
| 3 | Sheet naming | 7 side sheets are named `*Dialog`, which is why new code keeps treating them as modals | Rename to `*-sheet` (§6) |
| 4 | Card view | 69 tables offer a Cards toggle where tapping a card does nothing (0.1). Many card renderers also restate the list row | Fix 0.1; review whether Cards earns its place per page, or drop `viewToggle` cards where List already covers it |
| 5 | `loading.tsx` skeletons | Not audited here. AGENTS.md requires them to mirror `list="responsive"` when `defaultViewMode` is `{mobile:'contacts'}` | Run the existing UI audit tooling over `loading.tsx` after the list/sheet fixes land |
| 6 | Version history | My leave sheet changes earlier today shipped without a release-notes line | Added in this commit (`lib/version-history.ts`, Leave) |

# Part 2: whole-page review (sections 10–25)

Same method as Part 1: a parser read every `DataTablePage` (102), its `loading.tsx`, header
`actions`, tabs, stats, export and filters, then rules were applied. Sidebar labels come from
`admin-sidebar.tsx` and `sidebar.tsx`. Rows were spot-checked by hand; a parser bug that mapped
offices-rooms to the departments view was found that way and fixed.

| Section | Defaulters |
|---|---|
| 10. Whole-page scorecard (every page) | 102 pages |
| 11. Header actions (top right) | 35 |
| 12. Titles & descriptions | 32 |
| 13. Export & filters | 46 |
| 14. Skeletons (`loading.tsx` vs page) | 176 |
| 15. Card views | 114 tables (109 delete, 5 keep) |
| 16. Stats band | 98 |
| 17. Status colours | 14 |
| 18. Empty states | 25 |
| 19. Icon-only buttons | 5 |
| 20. Dates | 13 |
| 21. Destructive actions | 8 |
| 22. Dark-mode overrides | 24 |
| 23. Back links | 52 |
| 24. Delete / replace list | 12 |
| 25. Recommended order | — |

## 10. Whole-page scorecard

One row per table page, worst first. "Score" = defects found across §11–§16, §23, plus one each for
skeleton mismatches and a card view to delete.

| # | Route | File | Title | Header btns | Export | Tabs | Stats | Skeleton | Cards | Tables | Score |

## 11. Header actions (top right)

`DataTablePage` defaults to `actionsPlacement="inline-always"`: buttons sit beside the title and
**never wrap**, at every width.

| # | Route | File | Defect | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /directory | [directory-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/directory/directory-content.tsx) | 1. Manual "Refresh" button: TanStack Query already refetches on focus/invalidation | 1. Remove it; if data must be live, set `refetchInterval` on the query |
| 2 | /hr/leave | [leave-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/hr/leave/leave-content.tsx) | 1. 3 header buttons with no-wrap placement (inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown |
| 3 | /notifications | [notification-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/notifications/notification-content.tsx) | 1. 3 header buttons with no-wrap placement (default inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow<br>2. Manual "Refresh" button: TanStack Query already refetches on focus/invalidation<br>3. Admin tools (settings, managers) sit beside the page's primary action | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown<br>2. Remove it; if data must be live, set `refetchInterval` on the query<br>3. Group under a `Settings`/`More` menu so the primary action stands out |
| 4 | /reports/action-tracker | [page.tsx](../../app/(app)/reports/action-tracker/page.tsx) | 1. Manual "Refresh" button: TanStack Query already refetches on focus/invalidation | 1. Remove it; if data must be live, set `refetchInterval` on the query |
| 5 | /reports/weekly-reports | [page.tsx](../../app/(app)/reports/weekly-reports/page.tsx) | 1. 3 header buttons with no-wrap placement (inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown |
| 6 | /admin/assets | [admin-assets-content.tsx](../../app/admin/assets/admin-assets-content.tsx) | 1. 3 header buttons with no-wrap placement (default inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown |
| 7 | /admin/corporate-services/risk-register | [risk-register-view.tsx](../../app/admin/corporate-services/risk-register/_components/risk-register-view.tsx) | 1. 3 header buttons with no-wrap placement (default inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown |
| 8 | /admin/dev/login-logs | [dev-login-logs-content.tsx](../../app/admin/dev/login-logs/dev-login-logs-content.tsx) | 1. Manual "Refresh" button: TanStack Query already refetches on focus/invalidation | 1. Remove it; if data must be live, set `refetchInterval` on the query |
| 9 | /admin/dev/ui-errors | [ui-errors-content.tsx](../../app/admin/dev/ui-errors/ui-errors-content.tsx) | 1. 4 header buttons with no-wrap placement (default inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow<br>2. Manual "Refresh" button: TanStack Query already refetches on focus/invalidation | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown<br>2. Remove it; if data must be live, set `refetchInterval` on the query |
| 10 | /admin/hr/attendance | [view.tsx](../../app/admin/hr/attendance/view.tsx) | 1. 3 header buttons with no-wrap placement (default inline-always): on a phone they squeeze the title or overflow<br>2. Same label rendered twice (Reports, Attendance Manager, Export): mobile/desktop variants, or two buttons doing one job<br>3. Admin tools (settings, managers) sit beside the page's primary action | 1. One primary button (Add/New) + one secondary; everything else into a `⋯ More` dropdown<br>2. One button with a responsive label (`hidden sm:inline`)<br>3. Group under a `Settings`/`More` menu so the primary action stands out |

## 14. Skeletons

What you see as "two skeletons" is `loading.tsx` handing over to the page's own `DataTable`
skeleton rows. When the two disagree (stat count, tabs, buttons, table vs phone list), the layout
visibly changes mid-load. One page (`/admin/reports/action-tracker`) also nests a second full-page
skeleton in a `Suspense` fallback.

The biggest single cause: pages that open as a **phone list** (`defaultViewMode {mobile:"contacts"}`)
while `loading.tsx` draws a **table**. The fix is `list="responsive"` on those skeletons.

| # | Route | loading.tsx | Page | Mismatch | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /accounts/assets | [loading.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/assets/loading.tsx) | [assets-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/assets/assets-content.tsx) | 1. Skeleton draws 3 stat cards, page renders 4 | 1. `statCards={4}` |
| 2 | /accounts/payroll | [loading.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/payroll/loading.tsx) | [view.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/payroll/view.tsx) | 1. Header buttons: page 0, skeleton 1<br>2. Page opens as a phone list, skeleton draws a table → layout swaps on load<br>3. Page uses tight spacing, skeleton standard | 1. `actions={0}`<br>2. `list="responsive"`<br>3. `spacing="tight"` |
| 3 | /correspondence | [loading.tsx](../../app/(app)/correspondence/loading.tsx) | [portal-reference-generator-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/correspondence/portal-reference-generator-content.tsx) | 1. Header buttons: page 0, skeleton 1<br>2. Page has a back link, skeleton does not | 1. `actions={0}`<br>2. `showBackLink` |
| 4 | /documentation/department | [loading.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/department/loading.tsx) | [documentation-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/documentation-content.tsx) | 1. Page has a back link, skeleton does not<br>2. Page opens as a phone list, skeleton draws a table → layout swaps on load<br>3. Page uses tight spacing, skeleton standard | 1. `showBackLink`<br>2. `list="responsive"`<br>3. `spacing="tight"` |
| 5 | /documentation/personal | [loading.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/personal/loading.tsx) | [personal-documentation-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/documentation/personal/personal-documentation-content.tsx) | 1. Page has 2 tabs, skeleton 0<br>2. Header buttons: page 1, skeleton 2<br>3. Page has a back link, skeleton does not<br>4. Page opens as a phone list, skeleton draws a table → layout swaps on load<br>5. Filters: page 5, skeleton 2<br>6. Page uses tight spacing, skeleton standard | 1. `tabs={2}`<br>2. `actions={1}`<br>3. `showBackLink`<br>4. `list="responsive"`<br>5. `filters={5}`<br>6. `spacing="tight"` |
| 6 | /help-desk | [loading.tsx](../../app/(app)/help-desk/loading.tsx) | [help-desk-content.tsx](../../app/(app)/help-desk/help-desk-content.tsx) | 1. Header buttons: page 0, skeleton 2 | 1. `actions={0}` |

## 15. Should we have card views?

**Mostly no.** 114 tables offer Cards, and 113 of them also have the phone List. A
card is a third rendering of the same row, and on every expandable table tapping it does nothing (§0.1).
36 cards show nothing the columns don't. Keep cards only where they show something
a row can't (long text summaries, charts/progress). The toggle then becomes **List · Table**.

## 23. Back links

Labels in use today: "Back to Dashboard", "Back to Home", "Back to Admin", "Back to HR",
"Back to HR Dashboard", "Back to DEV", plain "Back" and more, for the same kinds of parent.
**Proposed rule:** pages the sidebar opens directly have no back link. Nested pages say
"Back to <parent's sidebar name>".

This **contradicts AGENTS.md's "Back link convention"**, which gives every `/admin/…` page a back
link. The convention has no recorded reason: it arrived in a bulk "sync uncommitted changes" commit
(`7283d7fb`). The best argument for it is that on a phone the sidebar is behind the menu button,
so a back link is one tap shorter. Against it: it costs a header line on every page, and the
labels have drifted apart (above). Your call; it is listed in §8.

Page is a sidebar destination, yet has a back link ("Back to Dashboard" → /profile): two navigation controls for one job, and a header row spent on it | 1. Remove the back link; keep back links for nested pages only |

## 24. Delete or replace

| # | What | Why | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `app/birthday1`, `app/birthday3`, `app/birthday4` | Design alternatives of `/birthday` (one is commented "Style option 3"). Admin-gated, linked from nowhere, still built and deployed | **Delete** all three |
| 2 | `TableExportContext` ([export-context.tsx](../../components/ui/data-table/export-context.tsx)) | The header export slot exists but nothing uses it, so the built-in export renders in the toolbar instead | Wire it into `DataTablePage` (so `export` lands in the header) **or delete it** |
| 3 | Three export systems | `ExportOptionsDialog` (21 pages), `ExportColumnsDialog` ×2 copies (`components/admin/` and `components/payments/`), and the built-in `DataTable` `export` (2 pages) | **Keep one.** The built-in `export` (filtered + sorted rows, no per-page code) in the header slot is the least code; then delete both `ExportColumnsDialog` copies |
| 4 | `cardRenderer` on ~110 tables | Third view mode that mostly repeats the list (§15) | **Delete** except the pages marked Keep; then simplify the toggle to List · Table |
| 5 | Synthetic 'band/window/coverage' filters (inventory, purchasing, bills, invoices) | Invented groupings ("Stock Band", "Due Window", "Item Volume") built for the generic template | **Replace** with real fields people search by |
| 6 | Manual "Refresh" header buttons | TanStack Query keeps data fresh | **Delete** (§11) |
| 7 | `components/documentation/admin-doc-view-dialog.tsx` | Modal copy of the staff doc-view sheet | **Delete**; reuse `doc-view-dialog` (sheet) |
| 8 | Staff vs admin duplicate screens (leave panel, action-tracker dialog, doc viewers) | Same UI built twice | **Replace** with one shared component each |
| 9 | `*Dialog`-named sheets (7) | Misleading names | **Rename** to `*-sheet` |
| 10 | `max-w-5xl`/`6xl` modals (employee view, manage users, bills/invoices forms) | Desktop-only sizes | **Replace** with sheet or page (§1, §7) |
| 11 | Hand-built action columns (payroll, impersonation, employees, roster…) | Buttons in a column instead of `rowActions` | **Replace** with `rowActions` |
| 12 | `test-results/ui-audit-report.json`, `ui-loading-report.json` | Generated by `npm run ui:audit`, yet tracked in git although `/test-results` is in `.gitignore`. Every audit run dirties the tree | **Untrack**: `git rm --cached test-results/*.json` |

---

## 25. Recommended order

1. **DataTable** (§0): sheet-first phone tap, sheet actions from `rowActions`, render-only columns in
   the sheet, flat bottom sheet, card tap. Then turn Cards off by default (§15).
2. **Skeletons** (§14): mechanical prop fixes, the largest being `list="responsive"`. Removes the
   mid-load jump on ~130 pages.
3. **Header actions** (§11): `More` menu pattern; move dialog markup out of `actions`; delete Refresh.
4. **One export system** (§13, §24).
5. **Titles to match the sidebar** (§12) and the back-link rule (§23): copy-only, low risk.
6. **Stats as line badges on phones** (§16).
7. **Modal → sheet moves** (§1) and the `DetailSheet` shell (§6).
8. Sweeps: status colours, empty states, dates, destructive styling, dark overrides (§17–§22).
9. Deletions (§24).


---

# Part 3: every page in the app (sections 26–35)

Covers all 280 `page.tsx` routes, not just table pages: 177 table, 10 dashboard, 18 detail, 7 form, 37 other, 11 redirect, 9 hub, 8 auth, 3 standalone.
Each page was read together with the components it renders (two import levels, excluding shared
`ui/` primitives), then classified by type and checked. Orphan candidates were confirmed by hand.
Most turned out to be linked through `md-desk/sections.ts` or sub-navigation and are **not** listed.
Suspected copy-paste pages (broadcast/reminders, tools/admin tools) were diffed. They share one
component, so they are **not** listed as duplicates.

| Section | Defaulters |
|---|---|
| 26. Every page: inventory and issue count | 269 pages |
| 27. Page shell and header | 7 |
| 28. Loading, empty and error states | 63 |
| 29. Browser tab titles | 220 |
| 30. Mobile layout risks | 62 |
| 31. Forms on pages | 24 |
| 32. Visual consistency (colours, text size) | 156 |
| 33. Accessibility | 103 |
| 34. Dead, duplicate and orphan pages | 8 |
| 35. Future-proofing | 11 |

## 28. Loading, empty and error states

Error boundaries exist for every route group (`app/error.tsx`, `(app)`, `admin`, `admin/pms`,
`pms`, `dept`) and `not-found.tsx` exists, so those are covered.

### 28a. Two skeletons in a row (page-level `PageLoader`)

Outside table pages, the "two skeletons" effect comes from `PageLoader`. The route's
`loading.tsx` draws one skeleton, then the client page draws `PageLoader` with different props
while its query loads.

| # | Route(s) | What happens | Effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /notifications/settings | `loading.tsx` → `FormPageSkeleton sections={3} fieldsPerSection={4} showSidebar`, then the page's `PageLoader variant="form"` → `FormPageSkeleton` with **defaults** | Two different form skeletons back to back |
| 2 | /tools/job-description | Hand-built `loading.tsx` (bare `Skeleton` bars), then `PageLoader variant="form"` | Two unrelated skeletons |
| 3 | /admin/hr/employees/[userId] | Hand-built `loading.tsx`, then `PageLoader` (`DetailPageSkeleton` defaults) | Two unrelated skeletons |
| 4 | /admin/accounts/bills/[id], /admin/accounts/invoices/[id], /admin/inventory/products/[id], /admin/purchasing/orders/[id], /admin/purchasing/suppliers/[id] | `loading.tsx` → `DetailPageSkeleton showSidebar sections={2}`, then `PageLoader` → `DetailPageSkeleton` **defaults** | Same component, different shape: visible jump |
| 5 | /accounts/payments/[id] (via `payment-detail-view.tsx`) | `PageLoader` defaults; route skeleton differs | Jump |

**Fix:** `PageLoader` takes the same props object as the route's `loading.tsx` (or the page
server-renders its first data and drops `PageLoader`).

## 29. Browser tab titles

There is no title template. The staff layout sets `"Dashboard | ACOB Lighting Technology Limited"`
and the admin layout `"Admin Dashboard | …"`, so every page below shows that in the browser tab,
history and bookmarks. Five open tabs all read "Dashboard".

| # | Route | Page | Tab shows today | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /accounts/assets | [assets/page.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/assets/page.tsx) | "Dashboard \| ACOB Lighting Technology Limited" | `export const metadata = { title: "<page title>" }` (with the template in §35 → "<page title> · Matrix") |

## 30. Mobile layout risks

| # | Route | Page | Defect | Solution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | /accounts/payments/[id] | [[id]/page.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/payments/[id]/page.tsx) | 1. 1676 lines rendered as one client tree: slow on low-end phones | 1. Split into server sections + small client islands |
| 2 | /accounts/requisitions/[id] | [[id]/page.tsx](../../app/(app)/accounts/requisitions/[id]/page.tsx) | 1. 2 raw `<Table>`: no phone layout | 1. DataTable (list rows on phones) or an `overflow-x-auto` wrapper for fixed breakdowns |
| 3 | /calendar | [calendar/page.tsx](../../app/(app)/calendar/page.tsx) | 1. 1 fixed pixel width(s) (`w-[NNNpx]`/`min-w-[NNNpx]`): overflow on a 375px phone<br>2. 2 grid(s) with 3+ columns and no breakpoint: squeezed on phones | 1. `w-full max-w-[…]` or responsive widths; email previews in an `overflow-x-auto` frame<br>2. `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-N` |
| 4 | /hr/attendance | [attendance/page.tsx](../../app/(app)/hr/attendance/page.tsx) | 1. 2 grid(s) with 3+ columns and no breakpoint: squeezed on phones | 1. `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-N` |

## 34. Dead, duplicate and orphan pages

| # | What | Finding | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `/birthday1`, `/birthday3`, `/birthday4` | Design alternatives of `/birthday`, linked from nowhere | **Delete** |
| 2 | `/admin/hr/leave/test` | "Leave Flow Test Harness" (with its own API route) shipped inside production admin; hand-built frame; linked from nowhere | **Delete**, or move under `/admin/dev/tests` behind the dev role |
| 3 | `/auth/sign-up` + `/auth/sign-up-success` | Public sign-up is disabled at Supabase (comment in `sign-up/page.tsx`), yet both pages remain, and `/auth/error` still shows a **Sign up** button linking to the disabled page | **Delete** sign-up-success; make `/auth/sign-up` redirect to login; remove the button on `/auth/error` |
| 4 | `/notifications/settings` **and** `/settings/notifications` | Two different notification-preference screens: per-module switches (linked from Notifications) vs a global email form (in Settings). People don't know which one controls what | **Merge** into `/settings/notifications` (two sections); redirect the other route |
| 5 | `/admin/pms/cbt/details` | "CBT Log Auditing" page linked from nowhere | Link it from `/admin/pms/cbt` or delete |
| 6 | `/admin/dev/tests` | Dev test page in production | Keep behind the dev role (check) or delete |
| 7 | Admin vs dept vs staff mirrors (`/dept/[dept_id]/…` re-exports ~60 admin pages) | Correct reuse. Every UI fix to an admin page also fixes its dept mirror; listed so nobody 'fixes' the mirrors separately | No change; fix once in the admin view |
| 8 | `/accounts/payments/[id]` + `@modal/(.)[id]` intercepting route | Payment detail exists as a full page and as an intercepted modal (614 lines) **and** as `payment-preview-sheet`: three surfaces for one record | Keep the sheet + full page; drop the intercepting modal |

---

## 35. Future-proofing: stop this coming back

The redesign only lasts if the defaults and the checks change too. Otherwise the next 50 pages
copy today's patterns.

| # | Area | Problem | Change |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Page types | Every page is implicitly one of: table, dashboard, detail, form, hub, auth, standalone. Nothing ties a type to its shell, width or skeleton, so they drift (§26–§28) | Add a **Page types** table to AGENTS.md: for each type its wrapper, `maxWidth`, header rules and `loading.tsx` skeleton. `ui:audit` checks a page against its type |
| 2 | Tab titles | No title template; group layouts hard-code "Dashboard \\| ACOB…" (§29); brand varies ("Matrix", "ACOB ERP", "ACOB Lighting Technology Limited") | Root layout: `title: { template: "%s · Matrix", default: "Matrix" }`; remove the group titles; `ui:audit` fails a page without `metadata` |
| 3 | Lint guardrails | Rules exist in AGENTS.md but nothing enforces them: tiny text, raw palette, `DialogContent` outside `ResponsiveModal`, `<img>`, `confirm()`, `key={index}`, `toLocaleString()` without locale | ESLint `no-restricted-syntax` / tailwind class regex rules for each, in `lint:strict` (already a zero-warning gate) |
| 4 | Ratchet, not big-bang | ~1,300 existing defaulters cannot all be fixed before the rules turn on | `ui:audit:baseline` already writes an allowlist: extend it to every rule above, commit the counts, and fail CI when any count **rises**. Counts only go down |
| 5 | Missing primitives | Each page hand-builds the same pieces: detail list, section heading, side-sheet shell, empty state, sticky form footer | Add `DetailList`/`DetailField`/`SectionHeading` (in `data-table/`), `DetailSheet`, `FormFooter`; then AGENTS.md says "use, don't rebuild" |
| 6 | Skeleton drift | Skeletons are hand-copied numbers (`statCards={4} tabs={3}`) that rot as pages change (§14, §28) | Generate them from the page: export the page's header/tabs/stats/filter config once and pass the **same object** to both the page and `loading.tsx`. Make `PageLoader` take the same props |
| 7 | `ui:audit` in the pipeline | `npm run ui:audit` exists but only runs in `check-all`; the pre-push hook skips it | Add it to pre-push next to `lint:strict` + `type-check` (it takes ~3s) |
| 8 | Visual regression | No screenshots: today's mobile overflow and skeleton jumps were only findable by reading code | Playwright: screenshot the 20 most-used pages at 375px and 1280px, light + dark, on each PR (diff only) |
| 9 | Status colours | 15 files keep local status→colour maps; 48 use `StatusBadge` | One status map in `StatusBadge`; lint bans `bg-*-100 text-*-700` pairs outside it |
| 10 | Card view / export / back links | Three decisions pending in §8 that every future page will copy | Decide them once, write them into AGENTS.md, update the `DataTable` defaults so the right thing is the default |
| 11 | Design review checklist | New pages are reviewed for types and lint, not for UI | PR template checkbox list: phone width checked · skeleton matches · empty state written · title matches sidebar · actions ≤2 + More |
